{});\n    const withEnv = deepMerge(merged, environment);\n\n    // Validate & freeze\n    const validated = zConfig.parse(withEnv);\n\n    this._config = deepFreeze(validated);\n\n    this.setupHotReload();\n  }\n\n  static instance(): ConfigManager {\n    if (!ConfigManager._instance) ConfigManager._instance = new ConfigManager();\n    return ConfigManager._instance;\n  }\n\n  /** Typed getter shortcut – guarantees immutability */\n  get config(): Readonly<Config> {\n    return this._config;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Hot reload configuration file (only in non-production and if override file exists).\n   * Emits 'reload' event on success.\n   */\n  private setupHotReload(): void {\n    const overridePath = process.env.PULSE_CONFIG_PATH;\n    if (!overridePath) return;\n    if (this._config.app.stage === 'production') return;\n\n    const absPath = path.isAbsolute(overridePath)\n      ? overridePath\n      : path.join(__dirname, overridePath);\n\n    fs.watch(\n      absPath,\n      { persistent: false },\n      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-misused-promises\n      async (_event, filename) => {\n        if (!filename) return;\n\n        try {\n          const newData = loadOverrideFile();\n          if (!newData) return;\n\n          const withEnv = deepMerge(deepMerge(defaults, newData), envToConfig());\n          const validated = zConfig.parse(withEnv);\n          this._config = deepFreeze(validated);\n\n          this.emit('reload', this._config);\n          // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n          console.info('[config] Hot-reloaded configuration');\n        } catch (err) {\n          // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n          console.error(\n            `[config] Failed to hot-reload configuration: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : err}`,\n          );\n        }\n      },\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public Export – as lazy getter function\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Retrieve the immutable configuration object\n *\n * Example:\n *   import { config } from '@/config';\n *   const port = config().server.port;\n */\nexport function config(): Readonly<Config> {\n  return ConfigManager.instance().config;\n}\n\n/**\n * Listen for hot-reload events (only emitted in non-production environments)\n *\n * Example:\n *   import { onConfigReload } from '@/config';\n *   onConfigReload((newCfg) => console.log('Config changed', newCfg));\n */\nexport function onConfigReload(listener: (cfg: Readonly<Config>) => void): void {\n  ConfigManager.instance().on('reload', listener);\n}\n```","numLines":398,"startLine":1,"totalLines":398}}}

this.logger.info('Correlation engine stopped.');\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Observer callback for infrastructure metrics.\n   */\n  onMetric(metric: InfraMetric) {\n    try {\n      if metric }, 'Metric appended to window.');\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger.error({ err, metric }, 'Failed to process metric.');\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Observer callback for social signals.\n   */\n  onSocialSignal(signal: SocialSignal) {\n    try {\n      if (!Number.isFinite(signal.value)) return;\n\n      this.socialWindow.append(signal.value, signal.timestamp);\n      this.signalType ??= signal.type;\n      this.logger.debug({ signal }, 'Social signal appended to window.');\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger.error({ err, signal }, 'Failed to process social signal.');\n    }\n  }\n\n  private evaluate() {\n    try {\n      if (\n        this.metricWindow.size() < this.config.minSamples ||\n        this.socialWindow.size() < this.config.minSamples\n      ) {\n        this.logger.debug(\n          'Not enough samples (%d/%d) – skipping evaluation.',\n          Math.min(this.metricWindow.size(), this.socialWindow.size()),\n          this.config.minSamples\n        );\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const x = this.metricWindow.values();\n      const y = this.socialWindow.values();\n\n      // Align lengths (in case sampling rates differ)\n      const len = Math.min(x.length, y.length);\n      const correlation = this.strategy.compute(\n        x.slice(-len),\n        y.slice(-len)\n      );\n\n      this.logger.debug(\n        {\n          correlation,\n          metricSamples: x.length,\n          socialSamples: y.length,\n        },\n        'Correlation evaluated.'\n      );\n\n      if (Math.abs(correlation) >= this.config.anomalyThreshold) {\n        const event: CorrelatedAnomalyEvent = {\n          correlationId: uuid(),\n          metricName: this.metricName ?? 'unknown_metric',\n          signalType:\n            this.signalType ?? SocialSignalType.LIVE_STREAM_VIEW,\n          correlationScore: correlation,\n          windowSize: len,\n          startTime: Date.now() - this.config.windowInSeconds * 1000,\n          endTime: Date.now(),\n          createdAt: Date.now(),\n        };\n\n        this.logger.warn(\n          { event },\n          'Correlation anomaly detected, publishing event.'\n        );\n        this.bus.publish<CorrelatedAnomalyEvent>('correlation.anomaly', event);\n      }\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger.error({ err }, 'Failed during correlation evaluation.');\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n//#endregion\n\n//#region ─────────────────────────────────────  Boot-strap helper  ───────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * Wiring function that registers the engine to the shared EventBus.\n * In production this happens in the DI container of the microservice.\n */\nexport function bootstrapCorrelationEngine(\n  bus: EventBus,\n  cfg: Partial<CorrelationEngineConfig> = {}\n): CorrelationEngine {\n  const defaultConfig: CorrelationEngineConfig = {\n    windowInSeconds: 60,\n    minSamples: 20,\n    anomalyThreshold: 0.85,\n    evaluationIntervalMs: 5_000,\n  };\n\n  const engine = new CorrelationEngine(\n    { ...defaultConfig, ...cfg },\n    new PearsonCorrelationStrategy(),\n    bus\n  );\n\n  // wire observers\n  bus.subscribe<InfraMetric>('metrics.ingest', (m) => engine.onMetric(m));\n  bus.subscribe<SocialSignal>('social.ingest', (s) => engine.onSocialSignal(s));\n\n  engine.start();\n  return engine;\n}\n\n//#endregion\n```","numLines":370,"startLine":1,"totalLines":370}}}

implements ThresholdStrategy {\n    81→  private readonly boostFactor: number;\n    82→\n    83→  constructor(boostFactor = 1.5) {\n    84→    this.boostFactor = boostFactor;\n    85→  }\n    86→\n    87→  compute(baseThreshold: number, event: SocialMetricEvent): number {\n    88→    // Simple heuristic: amplify threshold when delta exceeds 1k events/second.\n    89→    if (event.delta > 1_000) {\n    90→      const boosted = baseThreshold * this.boostFactor;\n    91→      logger.debug(`TrendingBoostStrategy applied: ${baseThreshold} -> ${boosted}`);\n    92→      return boosted;\n    93→    }\n    94→    return baseThreshold;\n    95→  }\n    96→}\n    97→\n    98→/**\n    99→ * Relaxes threshold during off-peak night hours to reduce false positives.\n   100→ */\n   101→export class NightTimeRelaxStrategy implements ThresholdStrategy {\n   102→  constructor(private readonly relaxFactor = 0.8) {}\n   103→\n   104→  private isNightHour(utcMillis: number): boolean {\n   105→ SocialMetricEvent): number {\n   110→    if (this.isNightHour(event.timestamp)) {\n   111→      const relaxed = baseThreshold * this.relaxFactor;\n   112→      logger.debug(`NightTimeRelaxStrategy (this.isNightHour(event.timestamp)) {\n      const relaxed = baseThreshold * this.relaxFactor;\n      logger.debug(`NightTimeRelaxStrategy applied: ${baseThreshold} -> ${relaxed}`);\n      return Implements the Strategy pattern so that\n *               SRE teams can hot-swap correlation algorithms at runtime (via dynamic\n *               configuration pushed through the platform’s configuration-management\n *               microservice).\n *\n *  Key Patterns:\n *      • Strategy Pattern      – Pluggable correlation algorithms\n *      • Observer (RxJS)       – Stream/buffer telemetry events\n *      • Command Pattern       – Publishes alert commands to message bus\n *      • Error-handling        – Fault-tolerant, production-grade\n *\n *  Author:      PulseSphere Core Engineering\n ****************************************************************************************/\n\nimport { Subject, Observable, bufferTime, filter, map } from 'rxjs';\nimport { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';\nimport dayjs from 'dayjs';\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                Domain Types                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/** Social context that accompanies every telemetry datapoint */\nexport interface SocialContext {\n  likes: number;\n  comments: number;\n  shares: number;\n  liveStreamViewers: number;\n  trendingHashtags?: string[];\n}\n\n/** Raw telemetry emitted by lower-level collectors */\nexport interface TelemetryEvent {\n  readonly id: string; // list of TelemetryEvent.id\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                     Infrastructure / Adapter Layer (Ports)                 */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Simplified interface for the platform’s configuration service.\n * It supports live-reloading and notifies subscribers on updates.\n */\nexport interface ConfigurationService {\n  get<T = unknown>(path: string): T;\n  onChange(path: string): Observable<unknown>;\n}\n\n/**\n * Outbound port for the event bus. In production this will be implemented\n * via NATS or Apache Kafka; we only need the contract for compilation.\n */\nexport interface MessageBus {\n  publish<T>(topic: string, message: T, headers?: Record<string, string>): Promise<void>;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                       Correlation Strategy – Abstraction                   */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Strategy pattern for correlation. Each implementation analyses the buffered\n * telemetry events and decides if/when an Alert must be raised.\n */\nexport interface AlertCorrelationStrategy {\n  /**\n   * Analyses a batch (time-window) of telemetry events and emits zero or more Alerts.\n   * @param events  – All telemetry events gathered within the window\n   */\n  correlate(events: ReadonlyArray<TelemetryEvent>): Alert[];\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*           Concrete Strategies – Used by AlertCorrelationEngine             */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Simple threshold-based strategy. Demonstrates how social amplification\n * (likes/shares) can raise severity.\n */\nexport class ThresholdStrategy implements AlertCorrelationStrategy {\n  private readonly cpuThreshold: number;\n  private readonly socialAmplificationFactor: number;\n\n  constructor(options: { cpuThreshold: number; amplificationFactor: number }) {\n    this.cpuThreshold = options.cpuThreshold;\n    this.socialAmplificationFactor = options.amplificationFactor;\n  }\n\n  correlate(events: ReadonlyArray<TelemetryEvent>): Alert[] {\n    const alerts: Alert[] = [];\n\n    events\n      .filter((e) => e.metric === 'cpu.utilization')\n      .forEach((event) => {\n        const amplification =\n          1 +\n          (event.social.likes + event.social.comments + event.social.shares) /\n            10_000; // amplify threshold by social heat\n\n        if (event.value >= this.cpuThreshold / amplification) {\n          alerts.push({\n            id: uuidv4(),\n            severity: amplification > this.socialAmplificationFactor ? 'CRITICAL' : 'WARN',\n            title: 'High CPU detected',\n            description: `CPU utilization ${event.value.toFixed(\n              2\n            )}% surpassed threshold (${this.cpuThreshold}%). Social amplification=${\n              amplification.toFixed(2)\n            }.`,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n            correlatedEvents: [event.id],\n          });\n        }\n      });\n\n    return alerts;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Trending-hashtag strategy. If an influencer surge is detected together with rising\n * latency metrics, promote the alert to CRITICAL.\n */\nexport class TrendingHashtagStrategy implements AlertCorrelationStrategy {\n  private readonly latencyP99ThresholdMs: number;\n  private readonly hashtagSurgeThreshold: number;\n\n  constructor(opts: { latencyP99ThresholdMs: number; hashtagSurgeThreshold: number }) {\n    this.latencyP99ThresholdMs = opts.latencyP99ThresholdMs;\n    this.hashtagSurgeThreshold = opts.hashtagSurgeThreshold;\n  }\n\n  correlate(events: ReadonlyArray<TelemetryEvent>): Alert[] {\n    const alerts: Alert[] = [];\n\n    const latencyEvents = events.filter((e) => e.metric === 'api.latency.p99');\n    const trendingEvents = events.filter(\n      (e) => e.social.trendingHashtags && e.social.trendingHashtags.length > 0\n    );\n\n    // If both latency high AND trending hashtags surging, create incident.\n    const hasLatencyIssue = latencyEvents.some((e) => e.value >= this.latencyP99ThresholdMs);\n    const trendingScore =\n      trendingEvents.reduce(\n        (acc, ev) => acc + (ev.social.trendingHashtags?.length ?? 0),\n        0\n      ) / Math.max(trendingEvents.length, 1);\n\n    if (hasLatencyIssue && trendingScore >= this.hashtagSurgeThreshold) {\n      const correlatedIds = [...latencyEvents, ...trendingEvents].map((e) => e.id);\n\n      alerts.push({\n        id: uuidv4(),\n        severity: 'CRITICAL',\n        title: 'Latency spike during trending hashtag surge',\n        description: `P99 latency exceeded ${\n          this.latencyP99ThresholdMs\n        }ms while trending activity score=${trendingScore.toFixed(\n          2\n        )}. Potential virality overload.`,\n        createdAt: Date.now(),\n        correlatedEvents: correlatedIds,\n      });\n    }\n\n    return alerts;\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                      Strategy Factory (Runtime Switch)                     */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntype StrategyDescriptor =\n  | { kind: 'threshold'; params: { cpuThreshold: number; amplificationFactor: number } }\n  | { kind: 'trending'; params: { latencyP99ThresholdMs: number; hashtagSurgeThreshold: number } };\n\n/**\n * Simplistic factory that instantiates correlation strategies based on config.\n * This allows runtime re-configuration without downtime.\n */\nexport class StrategyFactory {\n  public static create(descriptor: StrategyDescriptor): AlertCorrelationStrategy {\n    switch (descriptor.kind) {\n      case 'threshold':\n        return new ThresholdStrategy(descriptor.params);\n\n      case 'trending':\n        return new TrendingHashtagStrategy(descriptor.params);\n\n      default:\n        // TypeScript exhaustive check\n        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/restrict-template-expressions\n        throw new Error(`Unsupported strategy: ${(descriptor as any).kind}`);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                        Alert Correlation Engine (Core)                     */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ninterface EngineOptions {\n  bufferInMs: number; // Size of window for buffering telemetry events\n  telemetryStream$: Observable<TelemetryEvent>;\n  configService: ConfigurationService;\n  bus: MessageBus;\n  logger?: (msg: string, meta?: unknown) => void;\n}\n\n/**\n * Main engine responsible for:\n *   1. Buffering/aggregating telemetry events.\n *   2. Delegating to current correlation strategy.\n *   3. Publishing resulting alerts to the message bus.\n *   4. Reacting to dynamic configuration changes.\n */\nexport class AlertCorrelationEngine {\n  private readonly telemetry$ = new Subject<TelemetryEvent>();\n  private currentStrategy: AlertCorrelationStrategy;\n  private readonly strategyConfigPath = 'alerting.correlation.strategy';\n  private readonly logger: (msg: string, meta?: unknown) => void;\n\n  constructor(private readonly options: EngineOptions) {\n    this.logger = options.logger ?? console.log;\n\n    // Bootstrap initial strategy\n    const initialDescriptor = options.configService.get<StrategyDescriptor>(\n      this.strategyConfigPath\n    );\n    this.currentStrategy = StrategyFactory.create(initialDescriptor);\n\n    // Subscribe to config changes to hot-swap strategies\n    options.configService\n      .onChange(this.strategyConfigPath)\n      .subscribe((newDescriptor) => this.updateStrategy(newDescriptor as StrategyDescriptor));\n\n    // Wire telemetry stream\n    options.telemetryStream$.subscribe({\n      next: (ev) => this.telemetry$.next(ev),\n      error: (err) => this.logger('Telemetry stream failure', err),\n    });\n\n    // Start pipeline\n    this.initProcessingPipeline();\n  }\n\n  /** Hot-swaps the active strategy safely. */\n  private updateStrategy(descriptor: StrategyDescriptor): void {\n    try {\n      this.logger(`Switching correlation strategy → ${descriptor.kind}`);\n      this.currentStrategy = StrategyFactory.create(descriptor);\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger('Strategy swap failed, keeping previous strategy', err);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Builds the RxJS pipeline in charge of buffering + correlation + publication. */\n  private initProcessingPipeline(): void {\n    this.telemetry$\n      .pipe(\n        bufferTime(this.options.bufferInMs), // create tumbling window\n        filter((batch) => batch.length > 0),\n        map((batch) => this.safeCorrelate(batch)) // catch errors inside correlation\n      )\n      .subscribe({\n        next: (alerts) => alerts.forEach((a) => this.publishAlert(a)),\n        error: (err) => this.logger('Correlation pipeline failure', err),\n      });\n  }\n\n  /** Wraps strategy.correlate() with error handling so that one bad batch doesn’t kill the stream. */\n  private safeCorrelate(batch: TelemetryEvent[]): Alert[] {\n    try {\n      return this.currentStrategy.correlate(batch);\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger('Correlation error', err);\n      return [];\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async publishAlert(alert: Alert): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await this.options.bus.publish<Alert>('alerts', alert, {\n        'x-correlation-id': alert.id,\n        'x-generated-at': dayjs(alert.createdAt).toISOString(),\n      });\n      this.logger(`Alert published (${alert.severity})`, alert);\n    } catch (err) {\n      // We can add retry/backoff logic here\n      this.logger('Failed to publish alert', { alert, error: err });\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                 Example Bootstrap (would live elsewhere in prod)           */\n/* -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Helper functions\n * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\n\nfunction CalculateScaleFactor(metric: EnrichedMetric): number {\n  // Simple proportional algorithm – can be replaced by ML model\n  const cpuFactor = metric.infra.cpu / 80;\n  const socialFactor = metric.social.trendingScore / 70;\n  return Math.max(cpuFactor, socialFactor, 1.0);\n}\n\nfunction determineSeverity(metric: EnrichedMetric): AlertSeverity {\n  const { cpu, errorRate } = metric.infra;\n  const { trendingScore } = metric.social;\n\n  if (cpu > 90 || errorRate > 20 || trendingScore > 90) {\n    return AlertSeverity.CRITICAL;\n  }\n  if (cpu > 80 || errorRate > 15 || trendingScore > 80) {\n    return AlertSeverity.HIGH;\n  }\n  if (cpu > 70 || errorRate > 10 || trendingScore > 70) {\n    return AlertSeverity.MEDIUM;\n  }\n  return AlertSeverity.LOW;\n}\n\n/**\n * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Kafka Consumer – transforms EnrichedMetric events into Alerts\n * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\n\nclass MetricConsumer {\n  private readonly kafka: Kafka;\n  private readonly consumer: Consumer;\n  private readonly topic = ConfigManager.instance.get('kafkaTopic');\n  private started = false;\n\n  constructor(private readonly alertChain: AlertHandler) {\n    this.kafka = new Kafka({ brokers: ConfigManager.instance.get('kafkaBrokers') });\n    this.consumer = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId: 'alert-orchestrator' });\n  }\n\n  async start(): Promise<void> {\n    if (this.started) return;\n    await this.consumer.connect();\n    await this.consumer.subscribe({ topic: this.topic, fromBeginning: false });\n\n    await this.consumer.run({\n      eachMessage: async ({ message }) => {\n        try {\n          if (!message.value) return;\n          const metric: EnrichedMetric = JSON.parse(message.value.toString('utf8'));\n\n          logger.debug('Metric received', { metric });\n\n          const severity = determineSeverity(metric);\n          if (severity === AlertSeverity.LOW) return; // Ignore noise\n\n          const alert = new Alert(metric.service, severity, metric, 'Auto-generated alert');\n          await this.alertChain.handle(alert);\n        } catch (err) {\n          logger.error('Failed to process metric message', { err });\n        }\n      }\n    });\n\n    this.started = true;\n    logger.info('Metric consumer started');\n  }\n\n  async stop(): Promise<void> {\n    if (!this.started) return;\n    await this.consumer.disconnect();\n    this.started = false;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n *  Bootstrap\n * -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n */\n\nasync function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {\n  // Build chain\n  const scaleOut = new ScaleOutHandler(new KubernetesHpaScaler());\n  const cachePurge = new CachePurgeHandler();\n  const notify = new NotificationHandler();\n\n  scaleOut.setNext(cachePurge).setNext(notify);\n\n  // Attach listeners\n  const natsPublisher = new NatsAlertPublisher();\n  scaleOut.attachListener(natsPublisher);\n  cachePurge.attachListener(natsPublisher);\n  notify.attachListener(natsPublisher);\n\n  // Start consuming metrics\n  const consumer = new MetricConsumer(scaleOut);\n\n  process.on('SIGINT', async () => {\n    logger.info('SIGINT received. Graceful shutdown.');\n    await consumer.stop();\n    process.exit(0);\n  });\n\n  try {\n    await consumer.start();\n  } catch (err) {\n    logger.error('Failed to start MetricConsumer', { err });\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n}\n\nbootstrap().catch(err => {\n  logger.error('Unhandled bootstrap error', { err });\n  process.exit(1);\n});\n```","numLines":427,"startLine":1,"totalLines":427}}}

   );\n    });\n\n    // Kafka\n    this.kafka = new Kafka({\n      clientId: 'pulse-sphere-anomaly-detector',\n      brokers: (process.env.KAFKA_BROKERS ?? 'localhost:9092').split(','),\n      logLevel: logLevel.ERROR,\n    });\n  }\n\n  async start(): Promise<void> {\n    const topic = process.env.TELEMETRY_TOPIC ?? 'telemetry.enriched';\n    const groupId =\n      process.env.CONSUMER_GROUP_ID ?? 'anomaly-detector-consumer';\n\n    this.consumer = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId });\n    await this.consumer.connect();\n    await this.consumer.subscribe({ topic, fromBeginning: false });\n\n    logger.info({ topic, groupId }, 'Kafka consumer connected');\n\n    await this.consumer.run({\n      eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n        try {\n          const event = JSON.parse(\n            payload.message.value!.toString('utf8'),\n          ) as MetricEvent;\n          this.detector.evaluate(event);\n        } catch (err) {\n          logger.warn({ err }, 'Invalid metric event received');\n        }\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  async stop(): Promise<void> {\n    if (this.consumer) {\n      await this.consumer.disconnect();\n      logger.info('Kafka consumer disconnected');\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n/*                            Utilities                               */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\nfunction getMaxSeverity(detections: DetectionResult[]): Severity {\n  const ranking: Severity[] = [\n    Severity.Low,\n    Severity.Medium,\n    Severity.High,\n    Severity.Critical,\n  ];\n  return detections.reduce<Severity>(\n    (prev, curr) =>\n      ranking.indexOf(curr.severity) > ranking.indexOf(prev)\n        ? curr.severity\n        : prev,\n    Severity.Low,\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Simulates network I/O latency for stubbed external calls.\n */\nasync function fakeNetworkDelay(): Promise<void> {\n  return new Promise((res) =>\n    setTimeout(res, Math.random() * 200 + 100 /* 100–300ms */),\n  );\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n/*                       Module singleton export                      */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\nexport const anomalyDetectionService = new AnomalyDetectionService();\n\n/**\n * Auto-start when executed directly (useful for Docker entrypoints):\n *\n *   node dist/module_29.js\n */\nif (require.main === module) {\n  anomalyDetectionService\n    .start()\n    .then(() => {\n      logger.info('Anomaly detection service started');\n    })\n    .catch((err) => {\n      logger.fatal({ err }, 'Failed to start anomaly detection service');\n      process.exit(1);\n    });\n\n  // Graceful shutdown\n  process.on('SIGINT', async () => {\n    logger.info('Stopping anomaly detection service…');\n    await anomalyDetectionService.stop();\n    process.exit(0);\n  });\n}\n```","numLines":478,"startLine":1,"totalLines":478}}}

  43→\n    44→export enum ScalingDecision {\n    45→  SCALE_UP   = 'SCALE_UP',\n    46→  SCALE_DOWN = 'SCALE_DOWN',\n    47→  STABLE     = 'STABLE',\n    48→}\n    49→\n    50→/*          Strategy & Command Layer                         */\n    72→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    73→\n    74→/** Scaling command contract implements ScalingCommand {\n    81→  constructor(\n    82→    private readonly meshClient: ServiceMeshClient,\n    83→    private readonly appId: string,\n    84→    private readonly factor: algorithm; production code\n   113→ * would rely on predictive analytics or RL models.\n   114→ */\n   115→class HighCpuStrategy implements ScalingStrategy {\n   116→  constructor(\n   117→    private EnrichedMetric): ScalingCommand | undefined {\n   123→    if (metric.cpuPct >= this.highWaterMarkPct) {\n   124→      const factor = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(metric.cpuPct / 25));\n   125→      return public async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n   171→    if (this.nextHandler) {\n   172→      await this.nextHandler.handle(metric);\n   173→    }\n   174→  }\n   175→}\n   176→\n   177→/** Debug-level handler – just noisy logs in dev environments. */\n   178→class DebugAlertHandler extends BaseAlertHandler {\n   179→  async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n   180→    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {\n   181→      console.debug(\n   182→        `[DEBUG] Metric → cpu:${metric.cpuPct}% mem:${metric.memPct}% ` +\n   183→        `sentiment:${metric.sentimentScore.toFixed(2)} trending:${metric.trendingScore}`,\n   184→      );\n   185→    }\n   186→    await super.handle(metric);\n   187→  }\n   188→}\n   189→\n   190→/** Warning-level handler – triggers standard alert channel. */\n   191→class WarningAlertHandler extends BaseAlertHandler {\n   192→  async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n   193→    if ${metric.memPct}%`,\n   197→      );\n   198→    }\n   199→    await super.handle(metric);\n   200→  }\n   201→}\n   202→\n   203→/** Critical handler – immediate pager-duty. */\n   204→class CriticalAlertHandler extends BaseAlertHandler {\n   205→  async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n   206→    const isCrit = metric.cpuPct > 95 || metric.memPct > 95 || metric.trendingScore >= 0.95;\n   207→    if (isCrit) {\n   208→      await AlertChannel.pagerDuty(\n   209→        `🚨 CRITICAL: ${metric.appId} likely overloaded! ` +\n   210→        `cpu=${metric.cpuPct}% mem=${metric.memPct}% trending=${metric.trendingScore}`,\n   211→      );\n   212→    }\n   213→    await super.handle(metric);\n   214→  }\n   215→}\n   216→\n   217→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   218→/*                              Service-mesh client                           */\n   219→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   220→\n   221→class ServiceMeshClient {\n   222→  private readonly http: AxiosInstance;\n   223→\n   224→  constructor(\n   225→    readonly baseURL: string = process.env.SERVICE_MESH_BASE_URL ?? 'http://mesh-gateway',\n   226→    readonly timeout = 5_000,\n   227→  ) {\n   228→    this.http = axios.create({ baseURL: this.baseURL, timeout: this.timeout });\n   229→  }\n   230→\n   231→  /**\n   232→   * Scale an application up/down by \"factor\" replicas.\n   233→   * This interacts with the platform orchestrator through\n   234→   * the mesh sidecar.\n   235→   */\n   236→  public async scale(appId: string, factor: number): Promise<void> {\n   237→    try {\n   238→      await this.http.post(`/apps/${appId}/scale`, { factor });\n   239→      console.info(`[Scale] ${appId} scaled by ${factor}`);\n   240→    } catch (err) {\n   241→      console.error(`[Scale] Failed to scale ${appId}:`, err);\n   242→    }\n   243→  }\n   244→}\n   245→\n   246→/* Placeholder: in production we'd push to Slack webhook\n   253→    console.log(`[Slack] ${message}`);\n   254→  }\n   255→\n   256→  static async pagerDuty(message: string): Promise<void> {\n   257→    // Placeholder: integration key & routing will sit in vault\n   258→    console.log(`[PagerDuty] ${message}`);\n   259→  }\n   260→}\n   261→\n   262→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   263→/*                            Main Orchestrator Class                         */\n   264→/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   265→\n   266→export class SocialAwareAutoScaler {\n   267→  private readonly kafka: Kafka;\n   268→  private readonly meshClient = new ServiceMeshClient();\n   269→  private readonly eventBus = new MetricEventBus();\n   270→  private readonly strategies: ScalingStrategy[] = [];\n   271→  private readonly alertChain: AlertHandler;\n   272→  private isShuttingDown = false;\n   273→\n   274→  constructor(\n   275→    private readonly kafkaBrokers: string[] = (process.env.KAFKA_BROKERS ?? 'localhost:9092').split(','),\n   276→    private readonly groupId = 'social-auto-scaler',\n   277→    private readonly topic = 'pulse.metric.enriched.v1',\n   278→  ) {\n   279→    this.kafka = new Kafka({\n   280→      clientId: 'pulse-auto-scaler',\n   281→      brokers: this.kafkaBrokers,\n   282→      logLevel: KafkaLogLevel.NOTHING,\n   283→    });\n   284→\n   285→    /* Strategy registry */\n   286→    this.strategies.push(\n   287→      new HighCpuStrategy(this.meshClient),\n   288→      new SentimentSpikeStrategy(this.meshClient),\n   289→    );\n   290→\n   291→    /* Alert chain bootstrap */\n   292→    const debug   = new DebugAlertHandler();\n   293→    const warn    = new WarningAlertHandler();\n   294→    const crit    = new CriticalAlertHandler();\n   295→    debug.setNext(warn).setNext(crit);\n   296→    this.alertChain = debug; // entrypoint\n   297→\n   298→    /* Wiring observer */\n   299→    this.eventBus.onMetric(async (metric) => {\n   300→      await this.alertChain.handle(metric);\n   301→      await this.applyStrategies(metric);\n   302→    });\n   303→  }\n   304→\n   305→  /**\n   306→   * Initialize kafka consumer & begin event pump.\n   307→   */\n   308→  public async start(): Promise<void> {\n   309→    const consumer = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId: this.groupId });\n   310→    await consumer.connect();\n   311→    await consumer.subscribe({ topic: this.topic, fromBeginning: false });\n   312→\n   313→    console.info('[AutoScaler] started.');\n   314→\n   315→    await consumer.run({\n   316→      eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n   317→        if (this.isShuttingDown) {\n   318→          await consumer.disconnect();\n   319→          return;\n   320→        }\n   321→        const metric = this.parseMetric(payload);\n   322→        if (metric) this.eventBus.emitMetric(metric);\n   323→      },\n   324→    });\n   325→\n   326→    /* Graceful shutdown binding */\n   327→    process.once('SIGINT' , () => this.shutdown());\n   328→    process.once('SIGTERM', () => this.shutdown());\n   329→  }\n   330→\n   331→  private async applyStrategies(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n   332→    for (const strategy of this.strategies) {\n   333→      const cmd = strategy.evaluate(metric);\n   334→      if (cmd) {\n   335→        try {\n   336→          await cmd.execute();\n   337→          break; // one strategy acted – avoid conflicting actions\n   338→        } catch (err) {\n   339→          console.error('[AutoScaler] Command failed:', err);\n   340→        }\n   341→      }\n   342→    }\n   343→  }\n   344→\n   345→  /**\n   346→   * Converts Kafka payload into EnrichedMetric object with validation.\n   347→   */\n   348→  private parseMetric({ * PulseSphere SocialOps\n * -------------------------------------------------------\n * Module: module_17 – Social-Aware Auto-Scaler & Alerting\n *\n * This module wires together multiple classic patterns to\n * provide a production-grade, socially-aware auto-scaling\n * component.\n *\n * Patterns in use\n *  • Observer            – MetricEventBus (EventEmitter)\n *  • Strategy            – ScalingStrategy implementations\n *  • Command             – Scale{Up,Down}Command\n *  • Chain-of-Responsibility – AlertHandler chain\n *  • Event Driven        – Kafka consumer feeding the bus\n *\n * External deps\n *  • kafkajs             – High-throughput metric ingestion\n *  • axios               – Mock service-mesh HTTP client\n *\n * Author:  PulseSphere SRE Platform Team\n * -------------------------------------------------------\n */\n\nimport { EventEmitter } from 'events';\nimport { Kafka, EachMessagePayload, logLevel as KafkaLogLevel } from 'kafkajs';\nimport axios, { AxiosInstance } from 'axios';\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Static typings                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/** A single enriched metric coming from pulse-fusion pipeline. */\nexport interface EnrichedMetric {\n  readonly appId: string;\n  readonly cpuPct: number;               // 0-100\n  readonly memPct: number;               // 0-100\n  readonly sentimentScore: number;       // –1 .. +1\n  readonly trendingScore: number;        // 0 .. 1 (probability of virality)\n  readonly timestamp: number;            // epoch ms\n}\n\nexport enum ScalingDecision {\n  SCALE_UP   = 'SCALE_UP',\n  SCALE_DOWN = 'SCALE_DOWN',\n  STABLE     = 'STABLE',\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Observer Layer                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Central in-process event bus – translates Kafka messages into local events\n * that strategies/handlers can subscribe to without needing Kafka semantics.\n */\nclass MetricEventBus extends EventEmitter {\n  static readonly EVT_METRIC = 'metric';\n\n  emitMetric(metric: EnrichedMetric): void {\n    this.emit(MetricEventBus.EVT_METRIC, metric);\n  }\n\n  onMetric(listener: (metric: EnrichedMetric) => void): void {\n    this.on(MetricEventBus.EVT_METRIC, listener);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                           Strategy & Command Layer                         */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/** Scaling command contract (Command Pattern). */\ninterface ScalingCommand {\n  execute(): Promise<void>;\n}\n\n/** Concrete command – scale up by N replicas. */\nclass ScaleUpCommand implements ScalingCommand {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly meshClient: ServiceMeshClient,\n    private readonly appId: string,\n    private readonly factor: number,\n  ) {}\n\n  public async execute(): Promise<void> {\n    await this.meshClient.scale(this.appId, this.factor);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Concrete command – scale down by N replicas. */\nclass ScaleDownCommand implements ScalingCommand {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly meshClient: ServiceMeshClient,\n    private readonly appId: string,\n    private readonly factor: number,\n  ) {}\n\n  public async execute(): Promise<void> {\n    await this.meshClient.scale(this.appId, -this.factor);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Strategy contract – returns a Command or undefined when no action. */\ninterface ScalingStrategy {\n  evaluate(metric: EnrichedMetric): ScalingCommand | undefined;\n}\n\n/**\n * CPU-centric strategy – reacts when usage is beyond a threshold.\n * This is a simplistic threshold-based algorithm; production code\n * would rely on predictive analytics or RL models.\n */\nclass HighCpuStrategy implements ScalingStrategy {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly meshClient: ServiceMeshClient,\n    private readonly highWaterMarkPct = 75,\n    private readonly lowWaterMarkPct  = 35,\n  ) {}\n\n  evaluate(metric: EnrichedMetric): ScalingCommand | undefined {\n    if (metric.cpuPct >= this.highWaterMarkPct) {\n      const factor = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(metric.cpuPct / 25));\n      return new ScaleUpCommand(this.meshClient, metric.appId, factor);\n    }\n    if (metric.cpuPct <= this.lowWaterMarkPct) {\n      return new ScaleDownCommand(this.meshClient, metric.appId, 1);\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Sentiment spike strategy – anticipates virality before infrastructure\n * gets hammered. Combines sentiment & trending scores.\n */\nclass SentimentSpikeStrategy implements ScalingStrategy {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly meshClient: ServiceMeshClient,\n    private readonly threshold = 0.85,\n  ) {}\n\n  evaluate(metric: EnrichedMetric): ScalingCommand | undefined {\n    if (metric.trendingScore >= this.threshold && metric.sentimentScore > 0) {\n      // For viral events we aggressively over-provision.\n      return new ScaleUpCommand(this.meshClient, metric.appId, 3);\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                          Chain-of-Responsibility Layer                     */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ninterface AlertHandler {\n  setNext(handler: AlertHandler): AlertHandler;\n  handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void>;\n}\n\nabstract class BaseAlertHandler implements AlertHandler {\n  private nextHandler?: AlertHandler;\n\n  public setNext(handler: AlertHandler): AlertHandler {\n    this.nextHandler = handler;\n    return handler;\n  }\n\n  public async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n    if (this.nextHandler) {\n      await this.nextHandler.handle(metric);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** Debug-level handler – just noisy logs in dev environments. */\nclass DebugAlertHandler extends BaseAlertHandler {\n  async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n    if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {\n      console.debug(\n        `[DEBUG] Metric → cpu:${metric.cpuPct}% mem:${metric.memPct}% ` +\n        `sentiment:${metric.sentimentScore.toFixed(2)} trending:${metric.trendingScore}`,\n      );\n    }\n    await super.handle(metric);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Warning-level handler – triggers standard alert channel. */\nclass WarningAlertHandler extends BaseAlertHandler {\n  async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n    if (metric.cpuPct > 85 || metric.memPct > 85) {\n      await AlertChannel.slack(\n        `⚠️ High resource usage on app ${metric.appId}: ` +\n        `CPU ${metric.cpuPct}% / MEM ${metric.memPct}%`,\n      );\n    }\n    await super.handle(metric);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Critical handler – immediate pager-duty. */\nclass CriticalAlertHandler extends BaseAlertHandler {\n  async handle(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n    const isCrit = metric.cpuPct > 95 || metric.memPct > 95 || metric.trendingScore >= 0.95;\n    if (isCrit) {\n      await AlertChannel.pagerDuty(\n        `🚨 CRITICAL: ${metric.appId} likely overloaded! ` +\n        `cpu=${metric.cpuPct}% mem=${metric.memPct}% trending=${metric.trendingScore}`,\n      );\n    }\n    await super.handle(metric);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                              Service-mesh client                           */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nclass ServiceMeshClient {\n  private readonly http: AxiosInstance;\n\n  constructor(\n    readonly baseURL: string = process.env.SERVICE_MESH_BASE_URL ?? 'http://mesh-gateway',\n    readonly timeout = 5_000,\n  ) {\n    this.http = axios.create({ baseURL: this.baseURL, timeout: this.timeout });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Scale an application up/down by \"factor\" replicas.\n   * This interacts with the platform orchestrator through\n   * the mesh sidecar.\n   */\n  public async scale(appId: string, factor: number): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await this.http.post(`/apps/${appId}/scale`, { factor });\n      console.info(`[Scale] ${appId} scaled by ${factor}`);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(`[Scale] Failed to scale ${appId}:`, err);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                              Alerting helpers                              */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nclass AlertChannel {\n  static async slack(message: string): Promise<void> {\n    // Placeholder: in production we'd push to Slack webhook\n    console.log(`[Slack] ${message}`);\n  }\n\n  static async pagerDuty(message: string): Promise<void> {\n    // Placeholder: integration key & routing will sit in vault\n    console.log(`[PagerDuty] ${message}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                            Main Orchestrator Class                         */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport class SocialAwareAutoScaler {\n  private readonly kafka: Kafka;\n  private readonly meshClient = new ServiceMeshClient();\n  private readonly eventBus = new MetricEventBus();\n  private readonly strategies: ScalingStrategy[] = [];\n  private readonly alertChain: AlertHandler;\n  private isShuttingDown = false;\n\n  constructor(\n    private readonly kafkaBrokers: string[] = (process.env.KAFKA_BROKERS ?? 'localhost:9092').split(','),\n    private readonly groupId = 'social-auto-scaler',\n    private readonly topic = 'pulse.metric.enriched.v1',\n  ) {\n    this.kafka = new Kafka({\n      clientId: 'pulse-auto-scaler',\n      brokers: this.kafkaBrokers,\n      logLevel: KafkaLogLevel.NOTHING,\n    });\n\n    /* Strategy registry */\n    this.strategies.push(\n      new HighCpuStrategy(this.meshClient),\n      new SentimentSpikeStrategy(this.meshClient),\n    );\n\n    /* Alert chain bootstrap */\n    const debug   = new DebugAlertHandler();\n    const warn    = new WarningAlertHandler();\n    const crit    = new CriticalAlertHandler();\n    debug.setNext(warn).setNext(crit);\n    this.alertChain = debug; // entrypoint\n\n    /* Wiring observer */\n    this.eventBus.onMetric(async (metric) => {\n      await this.alertChain.handle(metric);\n      await this.applyStrategies(metric);\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Initialize kafka consumer & begin event pump.\n   */\n  public async start(): Promise<void> {\n    const consumer = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId: this.groupId });\n    await consumer.connect();\n    await consumer.subscribe({ topic: this.topic, fromBeginning: false });\n\n    console.info('[AutoScaler] started.');\n\n    await consumer.run({\n      eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n        if (this.isShuttingDown) {\n          await consumer.disconnect();\n          return;\n        }\n        const metric = this.parseMetric(payload);\n        if (metric) this.eventBus.emitMetric(metric);\n      },\n    });\n\n    /* Graceful shutdown binding */\n    process.once('SIGINT' , () => this.shutdown());\n    process.once('SIGTERM', () => this.shutdown());\n  }\n\n  private async applyStrategies(metric: EnrichedMetric): Promise<void> {\n    for (const strategy of this.strategies) {\n      const cmd = strategy.evaluate(metric);\n      if (cmd) {\n        try {\n          await cmd.execute();\n          break; // one strategy acted – avoid conflicting actions\n        } catch (err) {\n          console.error('[AutoScaler] Command failed:', err);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Converts Kafka payload into EnrichedMetric object with validation.\n   */\n  private parseMetric({ message }: EachMessagePayload): EnrichedMetric | undefined {\n    try {\n      if (!message.value) return;\n      const obj = JSON.parse(message.value.toString());\n\n      /* Lightweight validation */\n      const metric: EnrichedMetric = {\n        appId          : obj.appId,\n        cpuPct         : Number(obj.cpuPct),\n        memPct         : Number(obj.memPct),\n        sentimentScore : Number(obj.sentimentScore),\n        trendingScore  : Number(obj.trendingScore),\n        timestamp      : Number(obj.timestamp),\n      };\n\n      if (!metric.appId || Number.isNaN(metric.cpuPct)) {\n        throw new Error('Invalid metric shape');\n      }\n      return metric;\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.warn('[AutoScaler] Discarded malformed metric:', err);\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Shutdown hook – ensures consumer disconnect & drains inflight tasks.\n   */\n  private async shutdown(): Promise<void> {\n    if (this.isShuttingDown) return;\n    this.isShuttingDown = true;\n\n    console.info('[AutoScaler] Shutting down…');\n    // Additional cleanup (HTTP, etc.) could be awaited here.\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                     Module bootstrap (only when run directly)              */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nif (require.main === module) {\n  (async () => {\n    try {\n      const scaler = new SocialAwareAutoScaler();\n      await scaler.start();\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('[AutoScaler] Fatal:', err);\n      process.exitCode = 1;\n    }\n  })();\n}\n```","numLines":399,"startLine":1,"totalLines":399}}}

'MEDIUM'\n   171→            : 'LOW';\n   172→\n   173→        alerts.push({\n   174→          timestamp: Date.now(),\n   175→          severity,\n   176→          reason: `Metric ${ev.metric} on ${ev.host} outlier – z=${zScore.toFixed(\n   177→            2,\n   178→          )}, socialDelta=${ev.socialDelta}`,\n   179→          meta: { host: ev.host, metric: ev.metric, value: ev.value, zScore },\n   180→        });\n   181→      }\n   182→    }\n   183→\n   184→    if (alerts.length) {\n   185→      this.logger.info(`Z-Score detected ${alerts.length} alert(s)`);\n   186→    }\n   187→    return alerts;\n   188→  }\n   189→}\n   190→\n   191→/* ─────────────── Concrete Strategy: Static Thresholds w/ Social Amplification ──────────────── */\n   192→class ThresholdStrategy implements AnomalyDetectionStrategy {\n   193→  constructor(\n   194→    private readonly cpuThreshold: number,\n   195→    private readonly memThreshold: number,\n   196→    private readonly logger: Logger,\n   197→  ) {}\n   198→\n   199→  detect(window: ReadonlyArray<CombinedEvent>): AnomalyAlert[] {\n   200→    const alerts: AnomalyAlert[] = [];\n   201→\n   202→    for (const ev of window) {\n   203→      if (\n   204→        (ev.metric === 'cpu_load' && ev.value >= this.cpuThreshold) ||\n   205→        (ev.metric === 'mem_util' && ev.value >= this.memThreshold)\n   206→      ) {\n   207→        const severity: AnomalyAlert['severity'] =\n   208→          ev.socialDelta > 100 ? The module listens to:\n *                 • Kafka   – infrastructure metrics topic\n *                 • NATS    – social-signal subject (likes/comments/shares/streams)\n *               and produces:\n *                 • Alert events via the internal AlertPublisher (fan-out to PagerDuty, Slack…)\n *\n *  Architectural patterns showcased:\n *     • Observer Pattern – RxJS observable streams\n *     • Strategy Pattern – pluggable anomaly-detection algorithms\n *     • Chain-of-Responsibility – strategies executed until one raises an alert\n *\n *  NOTE: All concrete implementations are self-contained to keep the example runnable in\n *        isolation. Replace stubs with your own infrastructure-specific classes as needed.\n **************************************************************************************************/\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────── External Dependencies ───────────────────────────── */\nimport { Kafka, EachMessagePayload } from 'kafkajs';\nimport { connect, NatsConnection, Subscription } from 'nats';\nimport {\n  bufferTime,\n  filter,\n  map,\n  merge,\n  Observable,\n  Subject,\n  withLatestFrom,\n} from 'rxjs';\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────── Types ────────────────────────────────────────── */\nexport interface MetricEvent {\n  timestamp: number;       // epoch-ms\n  signal: 'like' | 'comment' | 'share' | 'view';\n  count: number;           // delta since previous sample\n  topic: string;           // hashtag / live-stream id / post id\n}\n\nexport interface CombinedEvent extends MetricEvent {\n  socialDelta: number;     // count of social-interaction deltas in the correlation window\n}\n\nexport interface AnomalyAlert {\n  timestamp: number;\n  severity: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH';\n  reason: string;\n  meta: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n/* ─────────────────────────────────────── Logger (local stub) ────────────────────────────────── */\nclass Logger {\n  constructor(private scope: string) {}\n  info(msg: string, ...rest: unknown[]): void  { console.info (`[INFO] [${this.scope}]`, msg, ...rest); }\n  warn(msg: string, ...rest: unknown[]): void  { console.warn (`[WARN] [${this.scope}]`, msg, ...rest); }\n  error(msg: string, ...rest: unknown[]): void { console.error(`[ERR ] [${this.scope}]`, msg, ...rest); }\n}\n\n/* ───────────────────────────────────── Alert Publisher (stub) ───────────────────────────────── */\nclass AlertPublisher {\n  constructor(private readonly cfg: Config['alerting'], private readonly logger: Logger) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Fan-out alert to any configured integrations (PagerDuty, Slack, etc.).\n   * In production this should be async/non-blocking to avoid back-pressure on detectors.\n   */\n  async publish(alert: AnomalyAlert): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      // Replace console with real integrations (HTTP POST, gRPC, etc.)\n      this.logger.warn(`Dispatching alert ⇒ ${alert.severity}: ${alert.reason}`, alert.meta);\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger.error('Failed to publish alert', err);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────── Runtime Configuration Loader ──────────────────────────────── */\nclass Config {\n  /* Centralised configuration shape */\n  public kafkaBrokers: string[];\n  public metricTopic: string;\n  public natsServers: string;\n  public socialSubject: string;\n\n  public bufferWindowMs: number;\n  public zScoreThreshold: number;\n  public zScoreWindow: number;\n  public cpuThreshold: number;\n  public memThreshold: number;\n\n  public alerting: {\n    slackWebhook?: string;\n    pagerDutyKey?: string;\n  };\n\n  /* Hydrate configuration from environment variables */\n  static loadFromEnv(): Config {\n    const cfg = new Config();\n    cfg.kafkaBrokers   = (process.env.PS_KAFKA_BROKERS ?? 'HIGH'\n            : Math.abs(zScore) > this.threshold * 1.5\n            ? 'MEDIUM'\n            : 'LOW';\n\n        alerts.push({\n          timestamp: Date.now(),\n          severity,\n          reason: `Metric ${ev.metric} on ${ev.host} outlier – z=${zScore.toFixed(\n            2,\n          )}, socialDelta=${ev.socialDelta}`,\n          meta: { host: ev.host, metric: ev.metric, value: ev.value, zScore },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (alerts.length) {\n      this.logger.info(`Z-Score detected ${alerts.length} alert(s)`);\n    }\n    return alerts;\n  }\n}\n\n/* ─────────────── Concrete Strategy: Static Thresholds w/ Social Amplification ──────────────── */\nclass ThresholdStrategy implements AnomalyDetectionStrategy {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly cpuThreshold: number,\n    private readonly memThreshold: number,\n    private readonly logger: Logger,\n  ) {}\n\n  detect(window: ReadonlyArray<CombinedEvent>): AnomalyAlert[] {\n    const alerts: AnomalyAlert[] = [];\n\n    for (const ev of window) {\n      if (\n        (ev.metric === 'cpu_load' && ev.value >= this.cpuThreshold) ||\n        (ev.metric === 'mem_util' && ev.value >= this.memThreshold)\n      ) {\n        const severity: AnomalyAlert['severity'] =\n          ev.socialDelta > 100 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM';\n\n        alerts.push({\n          timestamp: Date.now(),\n          severity,\n          reason: `Threshold exceeded: ${ev.metric}=${(ev.value * 100).toFixed(1)}%`,\n          meta: { host: ev.host, metric: ev.metric, value: ev.value, socialDelta: ev.socialDelta },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (alerts.length) {\n      this.logger.info(`ThresholdStrategy detected ${alerts.length} alert(s)`);\n    }\n    return alerts;\n  }\n}\n\n/* ───────────────────────────── Detector (Delegates to Strategies) ───────────────────────────── */\nclass SocialSentimentAnomalyDetector {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly strategies: AnomalyDetectionStrategy[],\n    private readonly alertPublisher: AlertPublisher,\n    private readonly logger: Logger,\n  ) {}\n\n  async analyze(window: ReadonlyArray<CombinedEvent>): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      for (const strategy of this.strategies) {\n        const alerts = strategy.detect(window);\n        for (const alert of alerts) {\n          await this.alertPublisher.publish(alert);\n          // Chain-of-Responsibility: stop evaluating subsequent strategies if one fires a HIGH alert.\n          if (alert.severity === 'HIGH') return;\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.logger.error('Failed to analyse window', err);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/* ─────────────────────────────────────── Stream Bootstrapper ───────────────────────────────── */\nexport async function startSentimentAnomalyDetector(): Promise<void> {\n  const cfg     = Config.loadFromEnv();\n  const logger  = new Logger('SentimentDetector');\n  const alerts  = new AlertPublisher(cfg.alerting, logger);\n\n  /* ––––– Kafka (Infrastructure Metrics) ––––– */\n  const kafka         = new Kafka({ clientId: 'pulsesphere-metrics', brokers: cfg.kafkaBrokers });\n  const metricCons    = kafka.consumer({ groupId: 'pulsesphere-metrics-group' });\n  await metricCons.connect();\n  await metricCons.subscribe({ topic: cfg.metricTopic, fromBeginning: false });\n\n  const metricStream$ = new Subject<MetricEvent>();\n  metricCons.run({\n    autoCommit: true,\n    eachMessage: async ({ message }: EachMessagePayload) => {\n      if (!message.value) return;\n      try {\n        const evt = JSON.parse(message.value.toString()) as MetricEvent;\n        metricStream$.next(evt);\n      } catch (err) {\n        logger.error('Unable to parse metric event', err);\n      }\n    },\n  });\n\n  /* ––––– NATS (Social Interaction Signals) ––––– */\n  const natsConn: NatsConnection = await connect({ servers: cfg.natsServers.split(',') });\n  const socialSub: Subscription  = natsConn.subscribe(cfg.socialSubject);\n\n  const socialStream$ = new Subject<SocialSignalEvent>();\n  (async () => {\n    for await (const msg of socialSub) {\n      try {\n        socialStream$.next(JSON.parse(msg.data.toString()) as SocialSignalEvent);\n      } catch (err) {\n        logger.error('Unable to parse social signal', err);\n      }\n    }\n  })().catch((err) => logger.error('NATS subscription loop terminated', err));\n\n  /* ––––– Stream Correlation / Windowing ––––– */\n  const combined$ = metricStream$.pipe(\n    withLatestFrom(socialStream$),\n    map(([metric, social]): CombinedEvent => ({\n      ...metric,\n      socialDelta: social.count,\n    })),\n  );\n\n  const windowed$ = combined$.pipe(\n    bufferTime(cfg.bufferWindowMs),\n    filter((bucket) => bucket.length > 0),\n  );\n\n  /* ––––– Instantiate Detector & Strategies ––––– */\n  const detector = new SocialSentimentAnomalyDetector(\n    [\n      new ZScoreStrategy(cfg.zScoreThreshold, cfg.zScoreWindow, logger),\n      new ThresholdStrategy(cfg.cpuThreshold, cfg.memThreshold, logger),\n    ],\n    alerts,\n    logger,\n  );\n\n  windowed$.subscribe({\n    next: (window) => detector.analyze(window),\n    error: (err) => logger.error('Windowed stream error', err),\n  });\n\n  process.on('SIGINT', async () => {\n    logger.info('Gracefully shutting down detector…');\n    await metricCons.disconnect().catch(() => void 0);\n    natsConn.close();\n    process.exit(0);\n  });\n\n  logger.info('SocialSentimentAnomalyDetector started — monitoring streams.');\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────── Auto-start when executed directly ───────────────────── */\nif (require.main === module) {\n  // tslint:disable-next-line: no-floating-promises\n  startSentimentAnomalyDetector().catch((err) => {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console\n    console.error('[FATAL] Failed to bootstrap SocialSentimentAnomalyDetector', err);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n```\n","numLines":340,"startLine":1,"totalLines":340}}}

{\n  private readonly strategies: EnrichmentStrategy[];\n\n  constructor(strategies: EnrichmentStrategy[]) {\n    super();\n    this.strategies = strategies;\n  }\n\n  async handle({ message, topic, partition }: EachMessagePayload): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const parsedBytes = message.headers?._parsedMetric;\n      if (!parsedBytes) throw new Error('Missing parsed metric in headers');\n\n      const metric: RawMetric = JSON.parse(parsedBytes.toString());\n\n      let enriched: EnrichedMetric | undefined;\n      for (const strat of this.strategies) {\n        enriched = strat.enrich(metric);\n        if (enriched) break;\n      }\n\n      // Guaranteed at least InfraLoadStrategy applies\n      if (!enriched) {\n        enriched = new InfraLoadStrategy().enrich(metric);\n      }\n\n      message.headers = {\n        ...message.headers,\n        _enrichedMetric: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(enriched)),\n      };\n\n      await this.nextHandle({ message, topic, partition });\n    } catch (err) {\n      logger.error({ err }, 'Enrichment failure – routing to DLQ');\n      await DLQPublisher.publish(message);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Step 3: Publish enriched metric downstream\n */\nclass PublishStep extends AbstractStep {\n  constructor(private readonly producer: Producer) {\n    super();\n  }\n\n  async handle({ message }: EachMessagePayload): Promise<void> {\n    const enrichedBytes = message.headers?._enrichedMetric;\n    if (!enrichedBytes) {\n      logger.warn('No enriched metric found – skipping publish');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    await this.producer.send({\n      topic: CONFIG.enrichedMetricsTopic,\n      messages: [\n        {\n          value: enrichedBytes,\n          key: message.key,\n          headers: {\n            stratId: message.headers?.stratId || '',\n            sourceService: CONFIG.serviceName,\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n\n    // Optionally notify local observers\n    LocalEventBus.emit('metric.enriched', JSON.parse(enrichedBytes.toString()) as EnrichedMetric);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                  Observer Pattern – Internal Event Bus                     */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nclass LocalEventBus extends EventEmitter {}\nconst LocalEventBusSingleton = new LocalEventBus();\n\n/* Convenience re-export */\nexport const LocalEventBus = LocalEventBusSingleton;\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                          Dead Letter Queue Publisher                       */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nclass DLQPublisher {\n  private static producer: Producer;\n\n  static async init(kafka: Kafka) {\n    this.producer = kafka.producer({ allowAutoTopicCreation: true });\n    await this.producer.connect();\n  }\n\n  static async publish(failedMessage: EachMessagePayload['message']) {\n    if (!this.producer) throw new Error('DLQPublisher not initialised');\n\n    await this.producer.send({\n      topic: CONFIG.dlqTopic,\n      messages: [\n        {\n          key: failedMessage.key,\n          value: failedMessage.value,\n          headers: {\n            ...failedMessage.headers,\n            _failedAt: Buffer.from(Date.now().toString()),\n            _sourceService: Buffer.from(CONFIG.serviceName),\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                         Main Service Implementation                        */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nclass SocialContextEnricherService {\n  private readonly kafka: Kafka;\n  private readonly consumer: Consumer;\n  private readonly producer: Producer;\n  private readonly pipeline: PipelineStep;\n  private shuttingDown = false;\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.kafka = new Kafka({\n      clientId: CONFIG.serviceName,\n      brokers: CONFIG.kafkaBrokers,\n      logLevel: KafkaLogLevel.NOTHING,\n    });\n\n    this.consumer = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId: CONFIG.consumerGroupId, allowAutoTopicCreation: false });\n    this.producer = this.kafka.producer();\n    // pipeline wiring\n    const validation = new ValidationStep();\n    const enrichment = new EnrichmentStep([\n      new LiveViewerSpikeStrategy(),\n      new EngagementBurstStrategy(),\n    ]);\n    const publish = new PublishStep(this.producer);\n\n    validation.setNext(enrichment).setNext(publish);\n\n    this.pipeline = validation;\n  }\n\n  async start() {\n    await Promise.all([this.consumer.connect(), this.producer.connect(), DLQPublisher.init(this.kafka)]);\n\n    await this.consumer.subscribe({ topic: CONFIG.rawMetricsTopic, fromBeginning: false });\n\n    this.consumer.run({\n      autoCommit: true,\n      eachMessage: async (payload) => {\n        if (this.shuttingDown) return;\n        await this.pipeline.handle(payload);\n      },\n    });\n\n    this.handleProcessSignals();\n\n    logger.info(`SocialContextEnricherService started. Listening on topic \"${CONFIG.rawMetricsTopic}\"`);\n  }\n\n  private handleProcessSignals() {\n    const graceful = async () => {\n      if (this.shuttingDown) return;\n      this.shuttingDown = true;\n      logger.info('Shutting down gracefully…');\n      await Promise.all([this.consumer.disconnect(), this.producer.disconnect()]);\n      process.exit(0);\n    };\n\n    process.on('SIGTERM', graceful);\n    process.on('SIGINT', graceful);\n  }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                 Bootstrap                                  */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n  // Only run when executed directly (not when imported for tests)\n  const service = new SocialContextEnricherService();\n  service.start().catch((err) => {\n    logger.fatal({ err }, 'Fatal error while starting service');\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n```\n","numLines":428,"startLine":1,"totalLines":428}}}

             // correlation score in range [0, 1]\n    readonly riskLevel: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL';\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n *  Observer Pattern\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nexport interface CorrelatedEventObserver {\n    onCorrelatedEvent(event: CorrelatedEvent): void | Promise<void>;\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n *  Correlation Strategy Pattern\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nexport interface CorrelationStrategy {\n    /**\n     * Computes a correlation score between a metric and a social signal.\n     * Return in the inclusive range [0, 1].\n     *\n     * Returning NaN signals “unable to correlate”.\n     */\n    compute(\n        metric: SystemMetricEvent,\n        signal: SocialSignalEvent | null\n    ): number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Default implementation that gives higher weight to memory/cpu spikes during high\n * magnitude social events.\n */\nexport class WeightedCorrelationStrategy implements CorrelationStrategy {\n    compute(\n        metric: SystemMetricEvent,\n        signal: SocialSignalEvent | null\n    ): number {\n        if (!signal) return 0;\n\n        const metricFactor =\n            metric.metric.startsWith('cpu') || metric.metric.startsWith('memory')\n                ? 0.6\n                : 0.4;\n\n        // Normalise values: naive min-max assumption for demo purposes.\n        const normalisedMetric = Math.min(metric.value / 100, 1); // e.g. 90% CPU = 0.9\n        const normalisedSocial = Math.min(signal.magnitude / 10_000, 1);\n\n        const score = metricFactor * normalisedMetric + (1 - metricFactor) * normalisedSocial;\n\n        return Number.isNaN(score) ? 0 : Math.min(score, 1);\n    }\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n *  Chain-of-Responsibility for CorrelatedEvent processing\n * ========================================================================== */\n\ninterface PipelineStep {\n    handle(event: CorrelatedEvent, next: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void>;\n}\n\nclass ValidationStep implements PipelineStep {\n    private readonly ajv = new Ajv({ allErrors: true, removeAdditional: true });\n    private readonly validator: ValidateFunction<CorrelatedEvent>;\n\n    constructor() {\n        const schema = {\n            type: 'object',\n            required: [\n                'timestamp',\n                'metricHost',\n                'metricName',\n                'metricValue',\n                'score',\n                'riskLevel'\n            ]\n            // Additional JSON schema details intentionally omitted for brevity\n        };\n        this.validator = this.ajv.compile(schema);\n    }\n\n    async handle(event: CorrelatedEvent, next: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n        if (!this.validator(event)) {\n            throw new Error(\n                `CorrelatedEvent validation failed: ${this.ajv.errorsText(this.validator.errors)}`\n            );\n        }\n        await next();\n    }\n}\n\nclass EnrichmentStep implements PipelineStep {\n    async handle(event: CorrelatedEvent, next: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n        // Enrich riskLevel based on score.\n        const risk =\n            event.score >= 0.85\n                ? 'CRITICAL'\n                : event.score >= 0.6\n                ? 'HIGH'\n                : event.score >= 0.35\n                ? 'MEDIUM'\n                : 'LOW';\n\n        (event as any).riskLevel = risk; // Type cast for mutation inside pipeline.\n        await next();\n    }\n}\n\nclass PersistenceStep implements PipelineStep {\n    constructor(private readonly logger: winston.Logger) {}\n\n    async handle(event: CorrelatedEvent, next: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n        // In production we’d persist into ClickHouse / TimescaleDB or send to Kafka topic.\n        // For demo we just log; database errors should not break the pipeline.\n        try {\n            this.logger.info('Persisting correlated event', { event });\n        } catch (err) {\n            this.logger.warn('Failed to persist correlated event', { err, event });\n        }\n        await next();\n    }\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n *  CorrelationEngine — wiring everything together\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nexport interface CorrelationEngineConfig {\n    kafkaBrokers: string[];\n    metricsTopic: string;\n    socialTopic: string;\n    bufferWindowMs?: number; // default: 5_000\n}\n\nexport class CorrelationEngine {\n    private readonly metricStream$ = new Subject<SystemMetricEvent>();\n    private readonly socialStream$ = new Subject<SocialSignalEvent>();\n\n    private readonly observers = new Set<CorrelatedEventObserver>();\n    private readonly pipeline: PipelineStep[];\n\n    private readonly kafka: Kafka;\n    private readonly logger: winston.Logger;\n\n    private kafkaSubscriptions: Subscription[] = [];\n\n    constructor(\n        private readonly config: CorrelationEngineConfig,\n        private readonly strategy: CorrelationStrategy = new WeightedCorrelationStrategy(),\n        logger?: winston.Logger\n    ) {\n        this.logger =\n            logger ??\n            winston.createLogger({\n                level: 'info',\n                transports: [new winston.transports.Console()]\n            });\n\n        this.kafka = new Kafka({\n            clientId: 'pulsesphere-correlation-engine',\n            brokers: config.kafkaBrokers,\n            logLevel: KafkaLogLevel.ERROR\n        });\n\n        // Build pipeline once.\n        this.pipeline = [\n            new ValidationStep(),\n            new EnrichmentStep(),\n            new PersistenceStep(this.logger)\n        ];\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *\n     *  Public API\n     * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\n    start(): void {\n        this.logger.info('Starting CorrelationEngine …');\n\n        this.wireKafkaSubscriptions();\n        this.wireCorrelationStream();\n    }\n\n    stop(): void {\n        this.logger.info('Stopping CorrelationEngine …');\n        this.kafkaSubscriptions.forEach(sub => sub.unsubscribe());\n        this.metricStream$.complete();\n        this.socialStream$.complete();\n    }\n\n    registerObserver(observer: CorrelatedEventObserver): () => void {\n        this.observers.add(observer);\n        return () => this.observers.delete(observer);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *\n     *  Internal — Kafka consumption\n     * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\n    private wireKafkaSubscriptions(): void {\n        const consumerMetrics = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId: 'correlation-metrics' });\n        const consumerSocial = this.kafka.consumer({ groupId: 'correlation-social' });\n\n        const connectAndSubscribe = async (): Promise<void> => {\n            await consumerMetrics.connect();\n            await consumerSocial.connect();\n\n            await consumerMetrics.subscribe({\n                topic: this.config.metricsTopic,\n                fromBeginning: false\n            });\n            await consumerSocial.subscribe({\n                topic: this.config.socialTopic,\n                fromBeginning: false\n            });\n\n            await consumerMetrics.run({\n                eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n                    try {\n                        const event: SystemMetricEvent = JSON.parse(\n                            payload.message.value!.toString('utf8')\n                        );\n                        this.metricStream$.next(event);\n                    } catch (err) {\n                        this.logger.error('Failed to deserialize metric event', { err });\n                    }\n                }\n            });\n\n            await consumerSocial.run({\n                eachMessage: async (payload: EachMessagePayload) => {\n                    try {\n                        const event: SocialSignalEvent = JSON.parse(\n                            payload.message.value!.toString('utf8')\n                        );\n                        this.socialStream$.next(event);\n                    } catch (err) {\n                        this.logger.error('Failed to deserialize social event', { err });\n                    }\n                }\n            });\n        };\n\n        connectAndSubscribe().catch(err =>\n            this.logger.error('Kafka subscription failure', { err })\n        );\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *\n     *  Internal — Correlation logic\n     * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\n    private wireCorrelationStream(): void {\n        const bufferWindow = this.config.bufferWindowMs ?? 5_000;\n\n        // Merge both streams into correlated events every `bufferWindow`.\n        const correlated$: Observable<CorrelatedEvent> = merge(\n            this.metricStream$.pipe(map(e => ({ type: 'metric' as const, event: e }))),\n            this.socialStream$.pipe(map(e => ({ type: 'social' as const, event: e })))\n        ).pipe(\n            bufferTime(bufferWindow),\n            filter(bucket => bucket.length > 0),\n            map(bucket => this.correlateBucket(bucket))\n        );\n\n        // Subscribe once and dispatch to observers.\n        const sub = correlated$.subscribe({\n            next: event => this.dispatch(event),\n            error: err => this.logger.error('Correlation stream error', { err })\n        });\n\n        this.kafkaSubscriptions.push(sub);\n    }\n\n    private correlateBucket(\n        bucket: Array<\n            | { type: 'metric'; event: SystemMetricEvent }\n            | { type: 'social'; event: SocialSignalEvent }\n        >\n    ): CorrelatedEvent {\n        const metrics = bucket\n            .filter(b => b.type === 'metric')\n            .map(b => (b as any).event as SystemMetricEvent);\n        const socials = bucket\n            .filter(b => b.type === 'social')\n            .map(b => (b as any).event as SocialSignalEvent);\n\n        // Naively pick latest metric and social signal in this window.\n        const latestMetric =\n            metrics.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)[0] ??\n            ({\n                timestamp: Date.now(),\n                host: 'unknown',\n                metric: 'unknown',\n                value: 0\n            } as SystemMetricEvent);\n\n        const latestSocial =\n            socials.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)[0] ?? null;\n\n        const score = this.strategy.compute(latestMetric, latestSocial);\n\n        return {\n            timestamp: Date.now(),\n            metricHost: latestMetric.host,\n            metricName: latestMetric.metric,\n            metricValue: latestMetric.value,\n            socialSignal: latestSocial,\n            score,\n            riskLevel: 'LOW' // will be overwritten by EnrichmentStep\n        };\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *\n     *  Internal — Pipeline dispatch\n     * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n\n    private async dispatch(event: CorrelatedEvent): Promise<void> {\n        const invokePipeline = async (index: number): Promise<void> => {\n            if (index >= this.pipeline.length) return;\n            const step = this.pipeline[index];\n            await step.handle(event, () => invokePipeline(index + 1));\n        };\n\n        try {\n            await invokePipeline(0);\n            for (const obs of this.observers) {\n                try {\n                    await obs.onCorrelatedEvent(event);\n                } catch (err) {\n                    this.logger.warn('Observer threw error', { err });\n                }\n            }\n        } catch (err) {\n            this.logger.error('Pipeline processing failed', { err });\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* ============================================================================\n *  Example bootstrapping (only executed when module is entry point)\n * ========================================================================== */\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n    // Local dev bootstrap\n    const engine = new CorrelationEngine(\n        {\n            kafkaBrokers: process.env.KAFKA_BROKERS?.split(',') ?? ['localhost:9092'],\n            metricsTopic: 'telemetry.metrics.raw',\n            socialTopic: 'telemetry.social.raw',\n            bufferWindowMs: 3_000\n        },\n        new WeightedCorrelationStrategy()\n    );\n\n    // Simple console observer.\n    engine.registerObserver({\n        onCorrelatedEvent: evt => {\n            // Pretty-print only if riskLevel ≥ HIGH\n            if (['HIGH', 'CRITICAL'].includes(evt.riskLevel)) {\n                console.log('[🔥 INCIDENT CANDIDATE]', JSON.stringify(evt, null, 2));\n            }\n        }\n    });\n\n    engine.start();\n\n    process.on('SIGINT', () => {\n        console.log('Shutdown signal received — stopping engine …');\n        engine.stop();\n        process.exit(0);\n    });\n}\n```","numLines":426,"startLine":1,"totalLines":426}}}
