{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PV8KWzaxkbVzSvNKtGgSRt","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```typescript\n     2→/**************************************************************************************************\n     3→ * File:        src/module_2.ts\n     4→ * Project:     PulseSphere SocialOps (system_monitoring)\n     5→ * Description: Telemetry processing pipeline that enriches incoming infrastructure metrics with\n     6→ *              social-context signals (likes, comments, shares) and decides—via Strategy &\n     7→ *              Chain-of-Responsibility patterns—whether to orchestrate proactive capacity\n     8→ *              adjustments. The module exposes a self-contained `TelemetryProcessor` that can be\n     9→ *              wired into any event-driven transport (Kafka/NATS/HTTP) and notifies downstream\n    10→ *              observers when a scaling decision has been reached.\n    11→ *\n    12→ * Architectural patterns demonstrated:\n    13→ *   • Observer                  – EventEmitter-based subscription to processed telemetry\n    14→ *   • Chain-of-Responsibility   – Sequential handlers (validate → surge-detect → scale)\n    15→ *   • Strategy                  – Pluggable scaling algorithms (aggressive, conservative, noop)\n    16→ *\n    17→ * NOTE: This file purposefully avoids external framework bindings (NestJS, Express, etc.) to keep\n    18→ *       it transport-agnostic and easily testable. Integrations are expected to wrap the public\n    19→ *       `TelemetryProcessor` methods.\n    20→ **************************************************************************************************/\n    21→\n    22→import { EventEmitter } from 'events';\n    23→import { createLogger, format, transports, Logger } from 'winston';\n    24→\n    25→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    26→//  Domain Types\n    27→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    28→\n    29→/**\n    30→ * Represents an incoming telemetry datapoint produced by a system-metrics collector\n    31→ * and already enriched with social-engagement metadata upstream.\n    32→ */\n    33→export interface TelemetryEvent {\n    34→    metricName: string;                         // e.g. \"api.requests_per_sec\"\n    35→    value: number;                              // numeric datapoint\n    36→    timestamp: number;                          // epoch millis\n    37→    /** Social context signals harvested via the PulseSphere mesh sidecar */\n    38→    socialSignals: {\n    39→        likes: number;\n    40→        comments: number;\n    41→        shares: number;\n    42→        liveViewers: number;\n    43→    };\n    44→    /** Additional freeform metadata */\n    45→    meta?: Record<string, unknown>;\n    46→}\n    47→\n    48→/** Lightweight DTO emitted when a scaling request has been decided */\n    49→export interface ScalingCommand {\n    50→    readonly clusterId: string;\n    51→    readonly desiredReplicas: number;\n    52→    readonly reason: string;\n    53→    readonly issuedAt: number;\n    54→}\n    55→\n    56→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    57→//  Observer Pattern\n    58→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    59→\n    60→/**\n    61→ * Centralised broadcaster for processed events. Other bounded contexts (alerting,\n    62→ * deployment-automation, etc.) can listen to high-level signals without tight coupling.\n    63→ */\n    64→export class TelemetrySubject extends EventEmitter {\n    65→    static readonly EVENTS = {\n    66→        SCALING_NEEDED: 'scaling_needed',\n    67→        EVENT_REJECTED: 'event_rejected',\n    68→    } as const;\n    69→}\n    70→\n    71→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    72→//  Chain-of-Responsibility (abstract handler)\n    73→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    74→\n    75→/**\n    76→ * Base class for pipeline handlers. Each handler can decide to END or FORWARD the\n    77→ * processing. Errors bubble upward and are logged once by the pipeline coordinator.\n    78→ */\n    79→abstract class Handler {\n    80→    protected next?: Handler;\n    81→\n    82→    constructor(protected readonly logger: Logger) {}\n    83→\n    84→    setNext(handler: Handler): Handler {\n    85→        this.next = handler;\n    86→        return handler;\n    87→    }\n    88→\n    89→    async handle(event: TelemetryEvent, subject: TelemetrySubject): Promise<void> {\n    90→        const shouldContinue = await this.process(event, subject);\n    91→        if (shouldContinue && this.next) {\n    92→            return this.next.handle(event, subject);\n    93→        }\n    94→    }\n    95→\n    96→    /**\n    97→     * @returns true  → forward event to next handler\n    98→     *          false → stop chain propagation\n    99→     */\n   100→    protected abstract process(\n   101→        event: TelemetryEvent,\n   102→        subject: TelemetrySubject,\n   103→    ): Promise<boolean>;\n   104→}\n   105→\n   106→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   107→//  Concrete Handlers\n   108→// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   109→\n   110→/**\n   111→ * Performs basic schema & sanity checks. Rejects obviously corrupt data early.\n   112→ */\n   113→class ValidationHandler extends Handler {\n   114→    private static readonly REQUIRED_FIELDS: (keyof TelemetryEvent)[] = [\n   115→        'metricName',\n   116→        'value',\n   117→        'timestamp',\n   118→        'socialSignals',\n   119→    ];\n   120→\n   121→    protected async process(event: TelemetryEvent, subject: TelemetrySubject): Promise<boolean> {\n   122→        for (const field of ValidationHandler.REQUIRED_FIELDS) {\n   123→            if (event[field] === undefined || event[field] === null) {\n   124→                this.logger.warn(`Telemetry event missing required field \"${field}\"`, { event });\n   125→                subject.emit(TelemetrySubject.EVENTS.EVENT_REJECTED, {\n   126→                    reason: `Missing field: ${field}`,\n   127→                    event,\n   128→                });\n   129→                return false; // stop chain\n   130→            }\n   131→        }\n   132→        return true;\n   133→    }\n   134→}\n   135→\n   136→/**\n   137→ * Detects social-driven surges (likes, shares, etc.) that may soon overload\n   138→ * infrastructure. If a surge is not detected, the chain stops here.\n   139→ */\n   140→class SurgeDetectionHandler extends Handler {\n   141→    private readonly likeThreshold: number;\n   142→    private readonly shareThreshold: number;\n   143→    private readonly liveViewerThreshold: number;\n   144→\n   145→    constructor(logger: Logger, cfg?: { like?: number; share?: number; live?: number }) {\n   146→        super(logger);\n   147→        this.likeThreshold = cfg?.like ?? 1_000;\n   148→        this.shareThreshold = cfg?.share ?? 500;\n   149→        this.liveViewerThreshold = cfg?.live ?? 10_000;\n   150→    }\n   151→\n   152→    protected async process(event: TelemetryEvent): Promise<boolean> {\n   153→        const signals = event.socialSignals;\n   154→        const surgeDetected =\n   155→            signals.likes >= this.likeThreshold ||\n   156→            signals.shares >= this.shareThreshold ||\n   157→            signals.liveViewers >= this.liveViewerThreshold;\n   158→\n   159→        if (!surgeDetected) {\n   160→            this.logger.debug('No social surge detected, skipping scaling.', {\n   161→                metric: event.metricName,\n   162→                signals,\n   163→            });\n   164→            // Do NOT continue the chain – no scaling required\n   165→            return false;\n   166→        }\n   167→\n   168→        this.logger.info('Social surge detected.', {\n   169→            metric: event.metricName,\n   170→            signals,\n   171→        });\n   172→        return true; transports.Console()],\n            defaultMeta: { service: 'telemetry-processor' },\n        });\n\n        // Build handler chain\n        const validator = new ValidationHandler(this.logger);\n                // microservice name\n    readonly metric: string;                 // e.g. cpu_load, latency_p99\n    readonly value: number;                  // numeric value\n}\n\n/** Social interaction signal provided by the Engagement-Enricher service */\nexport interface SocialSignalEvent {\n    readonly id: string; baseline (average of previous window) – in production use PromQL or similar */\n    private previousAvgMap = new Map<string, number>();\n\n    evaluate(\n        metrics: ReadonlyArray<MetricEvent>,\n        signals: ReadonlyArray<SocialSignalEvent>,\n        config: AlertingEngineConfig,\n    ): Alert[] {\n        const alerts: Alert[] = [];\n\n        const groupedByServiceMetric: Record<string, MetricEvent[]> = {};\n        metrics.forEach((m) => {\n            const key = `${m.service}:${m.metric}`;\n            (groupedByServiceMetric[key] = groupedByServiceMetric[key] || []).push(m);\n        });\n\n        for (const [key, metricEvents] of Object.entries(groupedByServiceMetric)) {\n            const currentAvg =\n                metricEvents.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.value, 0) / metricEvents.length;\n\n            const prevAvg = this.previousAvgMap.get(key) ?? currentAvg;\n            this.previousAvgMap.set(key, currentAvg);\n\n            const deltaRatio = prevAvg === 0 ? 0 : (currentAvg - prevAvg) / prevAvg;\n\n            // simplistic spike detection\n            if (deltaRatio > 0.5) {\n                const [service, metric] = key.split(':');\n                const amplification = signals.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.weight, 0);\n\n                const severity =\n                    amplification > config.socialAmplificationThreshold\n                        ? AlertSeverity.CRITICAL\n                        : AlertSeverity.WARNING;\n\n                alerts.push({\n                    id: uuid(),\n                    triggeredAt: Date.now(),\n                    service,\n                    metric,\n                    severity,\n                    description: `Trend spike detected (${(\n                        deltaRatio * 100\n                    ).toFixed(1)}%) in ${metric}. Social amplification: ${amplification.toFixed(\n                        2,\n                    )}`,\n                    extraContext: { deltaRatio, amplification, windowSize: metricEvents.length },\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        return alerts;\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * Sentiment-based strategy: escalate if negative sentiment grows regardless of\n * infrastructure health (meta-monitoring).\n */\nexport class SentimentBasedAlertStrategy implements AlertStrategy {\n    evaluate(\n        metrics: ReadonlyArray<MetricEvent>,\n        signals: ReadonlyArray<SocialSignalEvent>,\n        config: AlertingEngineConfig,\n    ): Alert[] {\n        // Placeholder: suppose weight < 0 is negative sentiment multiplied by magnitude\n        const negativeWeight = signals\n            .filter((s) => s.weight < 0)\n            .reduce((acc, cur) => acc + Math.abs(cur.weight), 0);\n\n        if (negativeWeight === 0) {\n            return [];\n        }\n\n        // escalate in tiers\n        let severity = AlertSeverity.INFO;\n        if (negativeWeight > config.socialAmplificationThreshold * 0.5) {\n            severity = AlertSeverity.WARNING;\n        }\n        if (negativeWeight > config.socialAmplificationThreshold) {\n            severity = AlertSeverity.CRITICAL;\n        }\n\n        // pick an arbitrary metric as context – not relevant for sentiment\n        const sampleMetric = metrics[0];\n\n        return [\n            {\n                id: uuid(),\n                triggeredAt: Date.now(),\n                service: sampleMetric?.service ?? 'unknown',\n                metric: sampleMetric?.metric ?? 'n/a',\n                severity,\n                description: `Negative sentiment surge detected with aggregate weight ${negativeWeight.toFixed(\n                    2,\n                )}`,\n                extraContext: { negativeWeight },\n            },\n        ];\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------- Strategy Registry / Factory -------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport class StrategyRegistry {\n    private strategies: Map<string, AlertStrategy> = new Map();\n\n    constructor() {\n        // register built-ins\n        this.register('trend', new TrendBasedAlertStrategy());\n        this.register('sentiment', new SentimentBasedAlertStrategy());\n    }\n\n    register(name: string, strategy: AlertStrategy): void {\n        if (this.strategies.has(name)) {\n            throw new Error(`Strategy ${name} already registered`);\n        }\n        this.strategies.set(name, strategy);\n    }\n\n    resolve(name: string): AlertStrategy {\n        const strategy = this.strategies.get(name);\n        if (!strategy) {\n            throw new Error(`Strategy ${name} not found`);\n        }\n        return strategy;\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Social-Aware Alerting Engine (Observer + Strategy)                                            */\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nexport class SocialAwareAlertingService {\n    private subscription?: Subscription;\n    private readonly alertSubject = new Subject<Alert>();\n\n    constructor(\n        private readonly metricStream$: Observable<MetricEvent>,\n        private readonly socialStream$: Observable<SocialSignalEvent>,\n        private readonly configManager: ConfigManager,\n        private readonly strategyRegistry: StrategyRegistry,\n    ) {}\n\n    /**\n     * Start consuming streams into timed buffers for correlation */\n        const cfg = this.configManager.get();\n        const windowMs = cfg.correlationWindowMs;\n\n        this.subscription = merge(this.metricStream$, this.socialStream$)\n            .pipe(\n                bufferTime(windowMs),\n                filter((buffer) => buffer.length > 0),\n                map((buffer) => {\n                    const metrics: MetricEvent[] = [];\n                    const signals: SocialSignalEvent[] = [];\n                    buffer.forEach((e) => {\n                        if (isMetricEvent(e)) metrics.push(e as MetricEvent);\n                        else signals.push(e as SocialSignalEvent);\n                    });\n                    return { metrics, signals };\n                }),\n                tap(({ metrics, signals }) => {\n                    const effectiveConfig = this.configManager.get();\n                    const strategy = this.strategyRegistry.resolve(effectiveConfig.strategy);\n                    const alerts = strategy.evaluate(metrics, signals, effectiveConfig);\n                    alerts.forEach((alert) => this.alertSubject.next(alert));\n                }),\n            )\n            .subscribe();\n\n        /* Listen for dynamic configuration changes */\n        this.configManager.on('update', (newCfg: AlertingEngineConfig) => {\n            /* Simply log; the next buffer tick will fetch latest config automatically */\n            console.info(\n                `[SocialAwareAlertingService] Config updated: correlationWindowMs=${newCfg.correlationWindowMs}, strategy=${newCfg.strategy}`,\n            );\n        });\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Stop consuming streams gracefully.\n     */\n    stop(): void {\n        this.subscription?.unsubscribe();\n        this.subscription = undefined;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Expose alerts as observable for downstream consumers (PagerDuty, Grafana, etc.)\n     */\n    get alerts$(): Observable<Alert> {\n        return this.alertSubject.asObservable();\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Helper Type Guards The workflow spans 8 primary modules from rule ingestion through notification dispatch.\n\n## Key Files Identified\n\nThe following modules form the critical path for the alerting rule lifecycle:\n\n- `/app/project/src/module_36.ts` - **Configuration Ingestion & Management**: Manages alerting engine configuration with hot-reload capability\n- `/app/project/src/module_42.ts` - **Validation Pipeline**: Contains `ValidationStep` for schema validation using Zod\n- `/app/project/src/module_57.ts` - **Persistence Layer**: Contains `PersistenceStep` for storing correlated events\n- `/app/project/src/module_32.ts` - **Event Bus (Core Infrastructure)**: Defines the `EventBus` class that implements pub/sub communication\n- `/app/project/src/module_4.ts` - **Alert Correlation Engine**: Core engine that buffers telemetry and applies correlation strategies\n- `/app/project/src/module_29.ts` - **Anomaly Detection & Escalation**: Detects anomalies using pluggable strategies and triggers escalation chain\n- `/app/project/src/module_11.ts` - **Notification Dispatch**: Contains PagerDuty, Slack, and Email handlers for alert delivery\n- `/app/project/src/config.ts` - **Configuration Schema**: Defines core data structures and validation schemas\n\nSupporting files:\n- `/app/project/src/constants.ts` - Service names and Kafka topics enumeration\n- `/app/project/src/utils.ts` - Shared utilities including TTLCache for caching\n\n## Code Evidence\n\n### 1. Event Bus Definition (Core Pub/Sub Pattern)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_32.ts\n// Lines: 182-198\n\n/**\n * Internal EventBus abstraction.  In production we delegate to Kafka or NATS\n * but for local dev / unit testing an EventEmitter is sufficient.\n */\nexport class EventBus extends EventEmitter {\n  publish<T>(topic: string, payload: T) {\n    this.emit(topic, payload);\n  }\n\n  subscribe<T>(\n    topic: string,\n    listener: (payload: T) => void\n  ): () => void /* unsubscribe */ {\n    this.on(topic, listener);\n    return () => this.off(topic, listener);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Configuration Management with Hot-Reload\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_36.ts\n// Lines: 83-102\n\nexport class ConfigManager extends EventEmitter {\n    private current: AlertingEngineConfig;\n\n    constructor(defaults: AlertingEngineConfig) {\n        super();\n        this.current = defaults;\n    }\n\n    get(): AlertingEngineConfig {\n        return { ...this.current };\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Hot-reloads current configuration and notifies listeners\n     */\n    update(newConfig: Partial<AlertingEngineConfig>): void {\n        this.current = { ...this.current, ...newConfig };\n        this.emit('update', this.get());\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Validation Step (Chain-of-Responsibility)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_42.ts\n// Lines: 211-230\n\nclass ValidationStep extends AbstractStep {\n  async handle({ message, topic, partition }: EachMessagePayload): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      if (!message.value) throw new Error('Empty message');\n\n      const raw = JSON.parse(message.value.toString());\n      const parsed: RawMetric = RawMetricSchema.parse(raw);\n\n      // Pass parsed object inside message headers for downstream steps\n      message.headers = {\n        ...message.headers,\n        _parsedMetric: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(parsed)),\n      };\n      await this.nextHandle({ message, topic, partition });\n    } catch (err) {\n      logger.error({ err }, 'Validation failure – routing to DLQ');\n      await DLQPublisher.publish(message);\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Persistence Step\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_57.ts\n// Lines: 161-174\n\nclass PersistenceStep implements PipelineStep {\n    constructor(private readonly logger: winston.Logger) {}\n\n    async handle(event: CorrelatedEvent, next: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n        // In production we'd persist into ClickHouse / TimescaleDB or send to Kafka topic.\n        try {\n            this.logger.info('Persisting correlated event', { event });\n        } catch (err) {\n            this.logger.warn('Failed to persist correlated event', { err, event });\n        }\n        await next();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 5. Alert Correlation Engine (Metric Evaluation)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_4.ts\n// Lines: 241-269\n\nexport class AlertCorrelationEngine {\n  private readonly telemetry$ = new Subject<TelemetryEvent>();\n  private currentStrategy: AlertCorrelationStrategy;\n\n  constructor(private readonly options: EngineOptions) {\n    // Bootstrap initial strategy\n    const initialDescriptor = options.configService.get<StrategyDescriptor>(\n      this.strategyConfigPath\n    );\n    this.currentStrategy = StrategyFactory.create(initialDescriptor);\n\n    // Subscribe to config changes to hot-swap strategies\n    options.configService\n      .onChange(this.strategyConfigPath)\n      .subscribe((newDescriptor) => this.updateStrategy(newDescriptor as StrategyDescriptor));\n\n    // Wire telemetry stream\n    options.telemetryStream$.subscribe({\n      next: (ev) => this.telemetry$.next(ev),\n      error: (err) => this.logger('Telemetry stream failure', err),\n    });\n\n    // Start pipeline\n    this.initProcessingPipeline();\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 6. Notification Dispatch Chain (Chain-of-Responsibility)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_11.ts\n// Lines: 126-145, 150-180, 185-205\n\nabstract class EscalationHandler {\n  private nextHandler?: EscalationHandler;\n\n  setNext(handler: EscalationHandler): EscalationHandler {\n    this.nextHandler = handler;\n    return handler;\n  }\n\n  async handle(alert: Alert): Promise<void> {\n    const processed = await this.process(alert);\n    if (!processed && this.nextHandler) {\n      await this.nextHandler.handle(alert);\n    }\n  }\n\n  protected abstract process(alert: Alert): Promise<boolean>;\n}\n\nclass PagerDutyHandler extends EscalationHandler { /* ... */ }\nclass SlackHandler extends EscalationHandler { /* ... */ }\nclass EmailHandler extends EscalationHandler { /* ... */ }\n```\n\n### 7. Bootstrap Wiring - Notification Chain Assembly\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_11.ts\n// Lines: 381-385\n\n// Build escalation chain: PagerDuty → Slack → Email\nconst pagerDuty = new PagerDutyHandler(process.env.PAGERDUTY_KEY ?? 'dummy');\nconst slack = new SlackHandler(process.env.SLACK_WEBHOOK ?? 'https://hooks.slack.com/services/dummy');\nconst email = new EmailHandler(process.env.SMTP_ENDPOINT ?? 'https://smtp.internal');\npagerDuty.setNext(slack).setNext(email);\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Core Architectural Pattern: Event-Driven / Publish-Subscribe\n\nThe PulseSphere SocialOps platform uses an **Event-Driven Architecture** with **Publish/Subscribe** (pub/sub) as the primary decoupling mechanism. This is evidenced by:\n\n1. **EventBus Class** (`module_32.ts`): A central abstraction that wraps Node.js EventEmitter, providing `publish()` and `subscribe()` methods for topic-based communication.\n\n2. **Kafka/NATS Integration**: Multiple modules consume from Kafka topics and publish to NATS subjects, demonstrating distributed messaging patterns.\n\n3. **RxJS Observables**: Used extensively for reactive stream processing and event correlation.\n\n### Data Flow Sequence for Alerting Rule Lifecycle\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    ALERTING RULE DATA FLOW SEQUENCE                         │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  1. CONFIG INGESTION (module_36.ts)                                         │\n│     └─→ ConfigManager receives rule configuration                           │\n│         └─→ Emits 'update' event on hot-reload                              │\n│                                                                             │\n│  2. VALIDATION (module_42.ts)                                               │\n│     └─→ ValidationStep validates incoming config/metrics                    │\n│         └─→ Uses Zod schema parsing                                         │\n│         └─→ Invalid data routed to Dead Letter Queue                        │\n│                                                                             │\n│  3. PERSISTENCE (module_57.ts)                                              │\n│     └─→ PersistenceStep stores validated configuration                      │\n│         └─→ Persists to ClickHouse/TimescaleDB (production)                 │\n│                                                                             │\n│  4. EVENT BUS DISTRIBUTION (module_32.ts)                                   │\n│     └─→ EventBus publishes config/events to subscribers                     │\n│         └─→ Topic: 'metrics.ingest', 'social.ingest', 'correlation.anomaly' │\n│                                                                             │\n│  5. METRIC EVALUATION (module_4.ts)                                         │\n│     └─→ AlertCorrelationEngine buffers telemetry events                     │\n│         └─→ Applies configurable correlation strategies                     │\n│         └─→ ThresholdStrategy, TrendingHashtagStrategy                      │\n│                                                                             │\n│  6. ANOMALY DETECTION (module_29.ts)                                        │\n│     └─→ AnomalyDetector evaluates metrics against rules                     │\n│         └─→ Strategy Pattern: CpuSpikeStrategy, ViralSurgeStrategy          │\n│         └─→ Emits 'anomaly' events to observers                             │\n│                                                                             │\n│  7. ESCALATION CHAIN (module_29.ts)                                         │\n│     └─→ Chain-of-Responsibility: AutoScaler → AlertHandler → Commander      │\n│         └─→ Determines escalation path based on severity                    │\n│                                                                             │\n│  8. The system employs an **Event-Driven Architecture** with **Publish/Subscribe** patterns as the core decoupling mechanism. The workflow spans 8 primary modules from rule ingestion through notification dispatch.\n\n## Key Files Identified\n\nThe following modules form the critical path for the alerting rule lifecycle:\n\n- `/app/project/src/module_36.ts` - **Configuration Ingestion & Management**: Manages alerting engine configuration with hot-reload capability\n- `/app/project/src/module_42.ts` - **Validation Pipeline**: Contains `ValidationStep` for schema validation using Zod\n- `/app/project/src/module_57.ts` - **Persistence Layer**: Contains `PersistenceStep` for storing correlated events\n- `/app/project/src/module_32.ts` - **Event Bus (Core Infrastructure)**: Defines the `EventBus` class that implements pub/sub communication\n- `/app/project/src/module_4.ts` - **Alert Correlation Engine**: Core engine that buffers telemetry and applies correlation strategies\n- `/app/project/src/module_29.ts` - **Anomaly Detection & Escalation**: Detects anomalies using pluggable strategies and triggers escalation chain\n- `/app/project/src/module_11.ts` - **Notification Dispatch**: Contains PagerDuty, Slack, and Email handlers for alert delivery\n- `/app/project/src/config.ts` - **Configuration Schema**: Defines core data structures and validation schemas\n\nSupporting files:\n- `/app/project/src/constants.ts` - Service names and Kafka topics enumeration\n- `/app/project/src/utils.ts` - Shared utilities including TTLCache for caching\n\n## Code Evidence\n\n### 1. Event Bus Definition (Core Pub/Sub Pattern)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_32.ts\n// Lines: 182-198\n\n/**\n * Internal EventBus abstraction.  In production we delegate to Kafka or NATS\n * but for local dev / unit testing an EventEmitter is sufficient.\n */\nexport class EventBus extends EventEmitter {\n  publish<T>(topic: string, payload: T) {\n    this.emit(topic, payload);\n  }\n\n  subscribe<T>(\n    topic: string,\n    listener: (payload: T) => void\n  ): () => void /* unsubscribe */ {\n    this.on(topic, listener);\n    return () => this.off(topic, listener);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Configuration Management with Hot-Reload\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_36.ts\n// Lines: 83-102\n\nexport class ConfigManager extends EventEmitter {\n    private current: AlertingEngineConfig;\n\n    constructor(defaults: AlertingEngineConfig) {\n        super();\n        this.current = defaults;\n    }\n\n    get(): AlertingEngineConfig {\n        return { ...this.current };\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Hot-reloads current configuration and notifies listeners\n     */\n    update(newConfig: Partial<AlertingEngineConfig>): void {\n        this.current = { ...this.current, ...newConfig };\n        this.emit('update', this.get());\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Validation Step (Chain-of-Responsibility)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_42.ts\n// Lines: 211-230\n\nclass ValidationStep extends AbstractStep {\n  async handle({ message, topic, partition }: EachMessagePayload): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      if (!message.value) throw new Error('Empty message');\n\n      const raw = JSON.parse(message.value.toString());\n      const parsed: RawMetric = RawMetricSchema.parse(raw);\n\n      // Pass parsed object inside message headers for downstream steps\n      message.headers = {\n        ...message.headers,\n        _parsedMetric: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(parsed)),\n      };\n      await this.nextHandle({ message, topic, partition });\n    } catch (err) {\n      logger.error({ err }, 'Validation failure – routing to DLQ');\n      await DLQPublisher.publish(message);\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Persistence Step\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_57.ts\n// Lines: 161-174\n\nclass PersistenceStep implements PipelineStep {\n    constructor(private readonly logger: winston.Logger) {}\n\n    async handle(event: CorrelatedEvent, next: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n        // In production we'd persist into ClickHouse / TimescaleDB or send to Kafka topic.\n        try {\n            this.logger.info('Persisting correlated event', { event });\n        } catch (err) {\n            this.logger.warn('Failed to persist correlated event', { err, event });\n        }\n        await next();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 5. Alert Correlation Engine (Metric Evaluation)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_4.ts\n// Lines: 241-269\n\nexport class AlertCorrelationEngine {\n  private readonly telemetry$ = new Subject<TelemetryEvent>();\n  private currentStrategy: AlertCorrelationStrategy;\n\n  constructor(private readonly options: EngineOptions) {\n    // Bootstrap initial strategy\n    const initialDescriptor = options.configService.get<StrategyDescriptor>(\n      this.strategyConfigPath\n    );\n    this.currentStrategy = StrategyFactory.create(initialDescriptor);\n\n    // Subscribe to config changes to hot-swap strategies\n    options.configService\n      .onChange(this.strategyConfigPath)\n      .subscribe((newDescriptor) => this.updateStrategy(newDescriptor as StrategyDescriptor));\n\n    // Wire telemetry stream\n    options.telemetryStream$.subscribe({\n      next: (ev) => this.telemetry$.next(ev),\n      error: (err) => this.logger('Telemetry stream failure', err),\n    });\n\n    // Start pipeline\n    this.initProcessingPipeline();\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 6. Notification Dispatch Chain (Chain-of-Responsibility)\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_11.ts\n// Lines: 126-145, 150-180, 185-205\n\nabstract class EscalationHandler {\n  private nextHandler?: EscalationHandler;\n\n  setNext(handler: EscalationHandler): EscalationHandler {\n    this.nextHandler = handler;\n    return handler;\n  }\n\n  async handle(alert: Alert): Promise<void> {\n    const processed = await this.process(alert);\n    if (!processed && this.nextHandler) {\n      await this.nextHandler.handle(alert);\n    }\n  }\n\n  protected abstract process(alert: Alert): Promise<boolean>;\n}\n\nclass PagerDutyHandler extends EscalationHandler { /* ... */ }\nclass SlackHandler extends EscalationHandler { /* ... */ }\nclass EmailHandler extends EscalationHandler { /* ... */ }\n```\n\n### 7. Bootstrap Wiring - Notification Chain Assembly\n```typescript\n// File: /app/project/src/module_11.ts\n// Lines: 381-385\n\n// Build escalation chain: PagerDuty → Slack → Email\nconst pagerDuty = new PagerDutyHandler(process.env.PAGERDUTY_KEY ?? 'dummy');\nconst slack = new SlackHandler(process.env.SLACK_WEBHOOK ?? 'https://hooks.slack.com/services/dummy');\nconst email = new EmailHandler(process.env.SMTP_ENDPOINT ?? 'https://smtp.internal');\npagerDuty.setNext(slack).setNext(email);\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Core Architectural Pattern: Event-Driven / Publish-Subscribe\n\nThe PulseSphere SocialOps platform uses an **Event-Driven Architecture** with **Publish/Subscribe** (pub/sub) as the primary decoupling mechanism. This is evidenced by:\n\n1. **EventBus Class** (`module_32.ts`): A central abstraction that wraps Node.js EventEmitter, providing `publish()` and `subscribe()` methods for topic-based communication.\n\n2. **Kafka/NATS Integration**: Multiple modules consume from Kafka topics and publish to NATS subjects, demonstrating distributed messaging patterns.\n\n3. **RxJS Observables**: Used extensively for reactive stream processing and event correlation.\n\n### Data Flow Sequence for Alerting Rule Lifecycle\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    ALERTING RULE DATA FLOW SEQUENCE                         │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                                             │\n│  1.
